<div class="se-module se-module-text"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-1e19c688-fa31-499e-acc2-31a1b9fe5cad" style="line-height:1.8;"><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-8082b474-79e0-11f0-8a66-579cc09b797f" style=""> </span><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-8082b475-79e0-11f0-8a66-431ae2cf8ae7" style="color:#5ab03e;"> 안내 :: 저는 투자 경험이 많지 않은 초보 투자자입니다. 이 글은 특정 종목의 매수 또는 매도를 추천하는 글이 절대 아닙니다. 오직 제가 공부한 것을 기록하고, 훗날 제가 다시 보기 위한 목적으로만 작성된 개인적인 글입니다. 따라서 이 글의 내용을 투자 판단의 근거로 삼지 마시길 바라며, 모든 투자의 최종 책임은 투자자 본인에게 있습니다.</span></p></div>
</td></tr></tbody>
</table>

</div> <div class="se-component se-text se-l-default" id="SE-da0dbe23-a145-4e2f-a2b3-b4740157c467">
<div class="se-component-content">
<div class="se-section se-section-text se-l-default">
<div class="se-module se-module-text">
<!-- SE-TEXT { --><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-bcd61a63-9bd7-4d25-b3cc-f37b70884bb5" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-b2e3a4a7-f9a9-47c3-8d37-ff31e7432fbd" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-d29884ea-6a7d-4be8-b321-d7d6b4c7fdda" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs30 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-89dc239c-86b2-4068-9f04-e6ff20a68039" style="color:#1b1c1d;">4D Molecular Therapeutics (FDMT) 심층 분석 보고서: AAV 벡터 혁신을 통한 차세대 유전자 치료제 선점</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-1f22524c-b923-455f-88d6-c7cb85465480" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-602ef5a5-2fba-4549-b5db-1e87be059f04" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-0384def2-80b7-4836-9051-8b9ae76f0a11" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-075fbe02-c845-402c-b568-903d504dd230" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-63b21af6-7940-4ca3-9b12-707cef230216" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs24 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ac3c33ea-9ba6-4592-9215-2ec13075667d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">I. EXECUTIVE SUMMARY (경영진 요약)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-27285a6a-7382-4ab6-94d8-500ef5affc0c" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-db2b5f90-7fc1-4be8-914d-6a470a93ee52" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-2fe9e438-36ac-4dc5-8a42-9e556a0a5729" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-efcd5ce0-6776-4e05-8c6f-f36139230953" style="color:#1b1c1d;">투자 논지: 4D Molecular Therapeutics (FDMT)는 유전자 치료 분야의 고질적인 '전달(Delivery)' 문제를 해결한 핵심 플랫폼 기술 'Therapeutic Vector Evolution'을 보유, 아데노 연관 바이러스(AAV) 유전자 치료제 2.0 시대를 선도할 기업으로 평가됩니다. 동사의 기술적 해자는 경쟁사들이 직면한 안전성(염증 반응) 및 투여 편의성(수술 필요성) 문제를 근본적으로 해결한 맞춤형 AAV 캡시드(Capsid)에 있습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-b5e3c1c7-6855-4b6e-8712-c10b6131d6b2" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-2dced9ae-1cf5-40ce-9419-273e229bfbbf" style="color:#1b1c1d;">핵심 '탑픽' (3년 전망): 파이프라인 내 '탑픽'은 단연 **4D-150 (습성 황반변성, nAMD)**입니다. 이는 170억 달러 이상의 거대 시장 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-aaa2b346-7f39-4ae0-821f-051f5f2700c8" style="color:#444746;">1</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4feadc33-bf01-4bb6-81e4-55fce925d4fb" style="color:#1b1c1d;">을 타겟으로 하며, 1) 경쟁사 대비 압도적인 안전성 및 투여 편의성(외래 주사) </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b4fa80e6-64df-4241-abc5-c2d1ba9c50a2" style="color:#444746;">2</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-dbcc7cc4-e6ab-49fd-8410-e084304e87bb" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, 2) 2027년 하반기라는 명확한 3상 데이터 발표 일정 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-12a24bcc-6f48-471e-b422-35e94f2df5b5" style="color:#444746;">4</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-20d559dc-2e67-4ca8-ba85-0e7b33ec56e2" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, 3) Otsuka와의 대규모 라이선스 계약을 통한 상업적 가치 검증 및 재무적 리스크 해소 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1adf600c-5eac-4f45-92af-02514854ea1f" style="color:#444746;">5</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-208fceac-e8c1-4c84-b142-692b97607a09" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에 근거합니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-7255409d-0367-4fa7-bd7a-7174275863a1" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-90a7a841-cffc-44cb-b8af-14bc40daf7e1" style="color:#1b1c1d;">재무 건전성: 2024년 2월 약 3억 달러의 대규모 증자 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e1e70cf4-f1d2-4808-a877-775ef24dcfb0" style="color:#444746;">6</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a5257316-63c5-49d0-9fc4-2a14182ab075" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 및 2025년 10월 Otsuka로부터의 계약금 8,500만 달러 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a0b51bb2-3d24-4ccf-a1ec-6cab61795d90" style="color:#444746;">5</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-79057f15-d063-4aaf-9f94-54c9916dc951" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 확보로, 3상 데이터 발표 시점을 넘어 2028년까지의 현금 흐름(Cash Runway)을 확보했습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-bcc80620-fc8b-4f86-8603-c8767e20f068" style="color:#444746;">4</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0836a697-3aae-4d83-b7c7-3369d75cdda6" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 이는 임상 개발 후기 단계의 바이오텍에 흔히 발생하는 재무적 리스크(Financing Overhang)를 완벽히 제거한, 매우 강력한 투자 포인트입니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ad3435d9-9a49-4537-8e6d-44e1d7a14a68" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1fd9ebd8-be6f-4440-bace-7a0eb7732e31" style="color:#1b1c1d;">주요 리스크: 핵심 리스크는 4D-150의 3상 임상 실패입니다. 또한 938만 주에 달하는 사전 조달 신주인수권(Pre-funded Warrants) </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d7947eb8-a5f5-451f-801c-118523c7124a" style="color:#444746;">7</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9490c920-c338-416c-9196-a2b30887e042" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 및 약 485만 주의 스톡옵션 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-94f2d1f0-149a-44d3-bc36-b7441e4152d0" style="color:#444746;">8</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-389db02c-7e5d-4b79-b392-afb7b55b3e18" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에서 비롯되는 잠재적 주식 가치 희석(Overhang)은 주가에 부담 요인이나, 이는 이미 주주 구성에 반영된(사실상 내재화된) 물량으로 판단됩니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a7873ff5-f23d-4673-8106-2354d4f41412" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-adeb9320-1bcc-4758-a4c9-134b96766da7" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e5a8d759-9f69-4db0-976a-acb1dc2ac225" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs24 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-57b43c5f-c704-4124-9fc6-7b4297fe0780" style="color:#1b1c1d;">II. 기업 개요 및 비즈니스 모델</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a77cc76c-9eea-4bc3-9158-fba0b45d3f16" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-a8f20071-e08c-4913-84a6-320160d269db" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-5b146613-1587-4114-a454-0e0f3cc29043" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-a8993e77-276c-4af3-b474-e05f9e9b1c4f" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-fd433436-774d-478d-8b87-80b95bdccb64" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-428c615a-5aa5-4657-a727-a72d944d4949" style="color:#1b1c1d;">가. 4DMT는 무엇을 하는 회사인가: '유도 진화' 기반의 차세대 AAV 유전자 치료제</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-214b30b7-4f07-426c-aa66-34144de6f56f" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-c8b55a14-7b41-4c69-ab93-0c5c0ec894f0" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-5ac33a21-e60b-4f75-ab7c-09fa0e2814d9" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e0f1b97c-a8de-4c5e-8410-6ae00e242e5b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">4D Molecular Therapeutics (FDMT)는 임상 단계의 유전자 치료제 개발 기업으로, 기존 AAV (아데노 연관 바이러스) 벡터의 고질적인 한계를 극복하는 것을 핵심 목표로 삼고 있습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-2a4d7563-1e87-4915-8e7e-5dcf7e950ca8" style="color:#444746;">9</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-49a7b303-0f49-4bd7-8753-2ccdebe46acd" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 유전자 치료의 성패는 치료 유전자 자체보다, 그 유전자를 인체 내 원하는 조직 세포에 정확하고 안전하게 '전달'하는 능력에 달려있습니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e82fcad5-b1a8-4850-87a2-70f4813a8464" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ec670958-0612-42ad-bd0b-f1da99156b63" style="color:#1b1c1d;">FDMT의 비즈니스 모델은 노벨상 수상 기술인 '유도 진화(Directed Evolution)' </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-769d55d5-dd2b-4cca-a04d-ef6710a9785b" style="color:#444746;">10</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-da7e8e89-d2b1-48f6-8885-147c4143ca09" style="color:#1b1c1d;">를 AAV 캡시드(바이러스 껍데기) 개발에 적용한 독점 플랫폼 'Therapeutic Vector Evolution'에 기반합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-440b512e-6785-4d11-92b1-012f1eac211d" style="color:#444746;">11</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-51dec4a3-1538-4c9e-9309-128b0723dd62" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 이 플랫폼은 자연계에 존재하는 바이러스가 아닌, 약 10억 개의 합성 캡시드 라이브러리에서 특정 질병 및 표적 조직(예: 망막, 폐, 심장)에 유전자를 가장 효율적이고 안전하게 전달하도록 '설계'된 맞춤형 합성 벡터를 '발명'합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-97b2941f-bc04-48e1-9554-bf32db6f98b7" style="color:#444746;">10</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-0e115cb8-f5bf-4cec-8857-f1da07bb4772" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-830f614a-e0a8-4078-956e-2ae7bf6220a6" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-212c8ee3-94d7-444d-82a0-d7ff0049c9e9" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-51df774d-be92-49ec-adf0-8385b566d69b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">나. 핵심 비즈니스 모델: 내재화된 R&amp;D 엔진 및 전략적 라이선싱 (Dual-Pronged Strategy)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-9be28744-b765-4ffc-97c1-0be553cfd589" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-53e8e641-022d-49ef-a380-03eabb045d8a" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-38bfe8fe-2889-4f6a-b902-1cc222313b09" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f0b42ad1-7dde-469a-a02d-db9e8bb7126d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">FDMT는 두 가지 핵심 전략을 통해 수익을 창출하고 플랫폼 가치를 극대화합니다.</span></p><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-5c949e2e-e552-47a3-91de-d17d52fb6a0c" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f42be824-b235-4973-84be-fa8d0ef89ed9" style="color:#1b1c1d;">내부 파이프라인 개발: 안과(Ophthalmology), 폐(Pulmonology), 심장(Cardiology) 등 미충족 의료 수요가 크고 시장 규모가 거대한 분야에서 자체 신약 파이프라인을 개발합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-add81ad8-d100-44fa-9100-8c026a613089" style="color:#444746;">13</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-becb9609-bc3a-4dba-9a58-02fc02b71d69" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 이는 플랫폼 기술의 잠재력을 극대화하여 높은 상업적 수익을 창출하기 위한 핵심 전략입니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-271b9bc0-1221-45a0-83c5-9a60c83089e4" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-78d23174-f13a-4d01-8c3d-874311eb84f0" style="color:#1b1c1d;">플랫폼 기술 라이선스 아웃: 자체 개발한 핵심 벡터 기술(예: 안과용 R100 벡터)이나 특정 자산을 글로벌 제약사(Big Pharma)에 라이선스 아웃하여 단기 수익(계약금, 마일스톤)을 창출하고 R&amp;D 비용을 분담합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b2f89df7-f442-4820-8cc2-b9b1f3b0637c" style="color:#444746;">15</span></p></li></ol><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-7bf65ad2-6f82-4141-bba8-0c51b6213c5f" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-4ff052dc-d055-482c-9185-7a09b9bc3942" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-969c61cb-4f5d-47e1-81c2-4aed406dbb38" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0e733970-0b77-4510-96c6-f69395f1ea41" style="color:#1b1c1d;">다. 주요 고객: 미충족 의료 수요가 큰 환자, 의사, 그리고 빅파마</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-adf23c7b-c63f-4818-a2b1-ddd4be2d70cc" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-2e5384ba-5081-4ac5-afc9-35e0312f5dc1" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-0dc01591-39e0-4ff9-b6b3-1c63f1653b14" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-5a3ccf2c-4d78-41b2-9476-dd1c637246b9" style="color:#1b1c1d;">FDMT의 비즈니스 모델은 다층적인 고객을 대상으로 합니다.</span></p><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-6405d5bb-66ed-4447-a240-9195db7e7a61" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3b617091-0f50-4759-82a9-373fbb5a6563" style="color:#1b1c1d;">1차 고객 (End-User): 기존 치료 옵션이 없거나(예: CFTR 유전자 변이 낭포성 섬유증) </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a3873aef-a38b-4656-b25c-d57e192f9d00" style="color:#444746;">16</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9f37e8f6-2f5b-4ba9-8e06-d3a7222e038f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, 기존 표준 치료(Standard of Care)에 대한 '치료 부담(Treatment Burden)'이 극심한(예: 습성 황반변성 환자의 빈번한 안구 주사) </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1ab5c219-6a83-4bf2-86db-759ee76104bf" style="color:#444746;">1</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d1e9a8ef-3cbd-4002-a069-9f2601ab47d5" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 환자 집단입니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-cab120f6-6114-490b-af4d-8b17c7437286" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-46b0f2ad-1149-4fb9-95c5-91c18932eb92" style="color:#1b1c1d;">2차 고객 (Economic Buyer/Adopter):</span></p></li></ol><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ef1840d5-6c72-49e6-b2ab-52b36090058a" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0b2826f8-3183-40e8-b43b-fca171506cf7" style="color:#1b1c1d;">전문의 (Specialist Physicians): FDMT의 비즈니스 모델은 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-96a2cb31-d3c0-446e-a086-100b0a22bb42" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>의사의 채택 용이성</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0e23ed45-3d01-4b7d-8739-a05491d3a48a" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에 중점을 둡니다. 이는 4D-150의 설계에서 명확히 드러납니다. 현재 습성 황반변성의 표준 치료(SoC)는 안과 의사가 외래 진료실에서 수행하는 간단한 '유리체강 내 주사(IVT)'입니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-7481fae3-747f-4152-a005-826cb618298c" style="color:#444746;">3</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d80ef8ce-108a-424b-b5c2-09695ae05842" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 경쟁사인 REGENXBIO의 3상 후보물질(ABBV-RGX-314)은 '망막하 수술(Subretinal Surgery)'을 필요로 합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-22c924d4-5991-4f58-8483-78d7ecada75e" style="color:#444746;">3</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f45ac06e-5a41-4046-b5fc-65f2d6c31215" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 이는 복잡하고, 비싸며, 별도의 수술실과 전문 외과 의사를 요구합니다. 반면 4DMT의 4D-150은 R100 벡터의 우수성 덕분에 SoC와 동일한 'IVT 주사'로 투여됩니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-cc2ee39c-e0d4-4243-94e5-57054fa793be" style="color:#444746;">1</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-2cf97b58-6c49-45cf-b5cd-8f92f7c4dca8" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 이는 의사 입장에서 어떠한 추가적인 교육이나 장비, 수술실도 필요 없음을 의미합니다. 따라서 4D-150은 경쟁사 대비 상업적 채택(Commercial Adoption)에 있어 비교 불가능한 우위를 가집니다. 의사는 환자에게 더 나은(치료 횟수 감소) 동시에 자신에게 더 편한(동일한 시술) 옵션을 선택할 것입니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-5b70b0ce-fb47-441b-964d-56a3c6c17e97" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-35148c24-00ef-4ce6-809a-d650c781f9c9" style="color:#1b1c1d;">빅파마 (Big Pharma): FDMT의 플랫폼은 그 자체로 상품입니다. Pfizer </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6dd3c878-96c7-4042-ab48-519ef6966652" style="color:#444746;">20</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6b970b29-06da-416e-bbc3-1133ab1d4f5d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, Roche </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-48c75527-bdac-478a-9dc1-bfad00384a7b" style="color:#444746;">20</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a97627bb-f6fe-4c27-9cb4-a0b411ec3b3c" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, Astellas </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0086cbe1-882c-4524-a163-ef6747c40524" style="color:#444746;">21</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-75f3252d-531f-4bc8-a827-070c27fbb719" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, Otsuka </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3b600710-9a69-4b9f-b00b-70b3061996cc" style="color:#444746;">5</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-96d896f8-90f4-4d92-a526-b86d82b6cd5d" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 등 다수의 글로벌 제약사가 FDMT의 기술을 검증하고 라이선싱(또는 협업)했습니다. 이들은 FDMT의 기술을 자사의 신약 개발 파이프라인에 적용하고자 하는 핵심 고객입니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-36c99306-e95f-4be0-a4d3-f3909787788e" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-af776824-e984-4060-a59d-4101cbf963d8" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-1f6afb1c-2ab3-44d5-8b62-912b4b58e85b" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs24 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b65e4b29-7928-4f13-8172-19628988a4d8" style="color:#1b1c1d;">III. 재무 건전성 및 현금흐름 분석 (2022-2024)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-38f5cde4-bd94-4e36-8fe8-ebbff899095d" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-b46da135-4928-491d-8f36-afae9133bce1" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-bd958b91-2742-4d62-8d81-ed378ea2ef7a" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-e91b1366-01c8-45b8-86e4-94365f19a521" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-f587a5b5-7983-4adc-981c-d315bfdfd319" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-8f7d2c08-34f8-4774-b4e0-a0d93ac50d37" style="color:#1b1c1d;">가. 3개년 요약 재무 및 현금흐름 (2022-2024)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-d1b97f00-1c7f-4fec-bf35-1b4e1e50cbe0" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-317b0736-e911-42cd-a1da-45d02e8732bb" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-09057af3-69ff-4f21-a522-0aa212a47a72" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ab2f339f-ea6b-4ca0-b98b-74f1b7fee41c" style="color:#1b1c1d;">임상 단계 바이오텍인 FDMT는 제품 판매로 인한 지속적인 수익이 없으며, 재무 활동을 통한 자금 조달이 핵심입니다. 제공된 자료 내 3개년의 완전한 현금흐름표는 포함되어 있지 않으나 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-cd2e9d51-e9cf-4a4f-862d-fddba3a52f8d" style="color:#444746;">22</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-642bf7f4-a5e2-42af-939a-c80bd0b75176" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, 2024년 10-K 및 2023년 10-K의 데이터를 기반으로 재구성한 핵심 요약본은 다음과 같습니다. (단위: 1,000 USD)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e0f3b59c-29a9-4d33-a32e-5ca875a3f311" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-276c7fc8-75f7-40d9-a100-8f8424fa7683" style="color:#1b1c1d;">표 1: 4DMT 요약 재무 및 현금흐름 (2022-2024)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-de40f6b9-cb18-4d93-9d52-4f9dcda0295e" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-54acdee3-fd48-4adb-b9e1-004957ab7cc1" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><!-- } SE-TEXT -->
</div>
</div>

</div> <div class="se-component se-text se-l-default" id="SE-85eec939-4acb-445c-9a92-2c2b7e8d4871">
<div class="se-component-content">
<div class="se-section se-section-text se-l-default">
<div class="se-module se-module-text">
<!-- SE-TEXT { --><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ff40c75c-03cc-4185-bbca-36dd6ac51569" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-b6253f82-8d1f-441a-9df4-c59f28fe3dfb" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ee42bb89-4e90-4923-9331-11e1a2b2dace" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4547f2eb-5370-4de5-864c-ce8eb34fa72f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">나. 현금흐름 분석</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-e435953d-2e42-464d-90b3-750cd5d9a4f8" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-dcf9607b-cfa4-4a7f-af9c-27acafdd8136" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-0438d405-46a8-46b6-b3d3-20a3808ef940" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9ff7d59c-f7b6-40ef-9414-28d52c5cf02c" style="color:#1b1c1d;">영업 현금흐름 (OCF): 임상 단계 바이오텍의 전형적인 모습으로, R&amp;D 비용(2023년 97.1M USD, 2024년 187.8M USD) </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0b0f947f-c0dc-470d-a58a-eb614ecd1804" style="color:#444746;">25</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c5e5daa6-f31f-47ec-af37-dab77c1f0238" style="color:#1b1c1d;">의 급격한 증가로 인해 막대한 현금 소진(Cash Burn)이 발생하고 있습니다. 2023년의 20.7M USD 수익 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6d4750bb-06bf-4152-9be5-b11b618946d3" style="color:#444746;">26</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-abe22f38-02da-441f-b504-cf59b65bbc0e" style="color:#1b1c1d;">은 Astellas로부터 받은 계약금 20M USD </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0c49ed60-c463-4f96-99f6-b437b11a711f" style="color:#444746;">21</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a556ec20-ff27-4940-b883-f790bae6a556" style="color:#1b1c1d;">가 반영된 것으로, 이는 일회성이며 OCF는 본질적으로 R&amp;D 투자에 의해 마이너스 상태입니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-1795baac-5138-4b17-8646-b00b33ff5d87" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c8c1a345-2566-4032-8485-2c5620d9975c" style="color:#1b1c1d;">투자 현금흐름 (ICF): 재무 활동으로 조달한 현금을 단기 유가증권(Marketable Securities) 등에 투자하고, 이를 현금화하여 영업 활동(R&amp;D)에 사용하는 패턴이 반복됩니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-42ddbe90-6f57-4823-947d-a49db05fd487" style="color:#444746;">29</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-3253e4ad-d5bc-4ebc-9764-60cf8c4941d6" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ee31be29-7146-4dd4-abca-b1e0967bc9ed" style="color:#1b1c1d;">재무 현금흐름 (FCF): FDMT의 생명줄입니다. 2024년 2월의 300M USD </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9d09f647-e976-4425-8d90-46893085b055" style="color:#444746;">6</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3542bd2c-d4ce-4a67-97ea-17bb313196df" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, 2021년 118.8M USD </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b9c4b7ad-106f-4232-ae00-70f53c6ec62c" style="color:#444746;">30</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-5401ec1f-1f05-4803-be5a-650cefbdfc26" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, 2020년 IPO 222M USD </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-21d6cb9b-c9a4-41db-909d-bd61d0b6b2f8" style="color:#444746;">31</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9e432cf1-e1fb-4104-afde-601aef99d675" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 등, 대규모 유상증자를 통해 영업 적자를 상회하는 막대한 현금을 주기적으로 조달해왔습니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-9b904855-bfb4-48cc-be2b-cf5b2adce418" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-7a8c0d1e-a4ab-4852-b594-4ab0773e8cb4" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a5f71230-06b7-49d6-b2de-ebbce862acdf" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1276eb9a-9d14-44cd-8009-7622c4c2e616" style="color:#1b1c1d;">다. 유동성 및 현금 확보(Cash Runway): 가장 강력한 투자 포인트</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a3b86000-2c6f-45f2-957c-2b21f1dcce82" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-596a0058-9c6b-4980-9a59-67a4e823f379" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-c58e84bd-b5dd-44f4-8a51-b8e6df163611" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-63c80505-2384-403a-a759-f2e65b289315" style="color:#1b1c1d;">핵심 데이터: 2024년 12월 31일 기준, 현금, 현금성 자산, 유가증권은 **총 5억 6백만 달러 (약 6,700억 원)**입니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6f4a3e8d-43c9-4ccd-8d89-2e6b4be4fd6e" style="color:#444746;">4</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-fe305db6-38b9-444f-9780-308d62779024" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-fd15f29b-6336-4f16-97ad-323c35b86314" style="color:#1b1c1d;">업데이트: 2025년 10월, FDMT는 Otsuka Pharmaceutical과 4D-150의 아시아태평양(APAC) 지역 라이선스 계약을 체결, 계약금 8,500만 달러 및 향후 3년간 5,000만 달러 이상의 R&amp;D 비용 분담 약정을 확보했습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b0ab48bd-1094-47cf-a57f-5e8aa2bcfcbe" style="color:#444746;">5</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-f1db50dc-0a83-4a52-91ff-e0550f360d2f" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1e70b16c-2f32-48c8-8b3a-b465e5c03cfb" style="color:#1b1c1d;">결론: FDMT는 이 현금으로 2028년까지 운영이 가능하다고 공식 발표했습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-da2f6722-3f78-44c0-bbef-512f812ec343" style="color:#444746;">4</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-89dea9eb-f1c0-4929-a32c-95661867a963" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-343f2813-25b4-4fab-8aa0-a1d1bce10b23" style="color:#1b1c1d;">이는 FDMT가 '재무적 시간(Financial Runway)'을 확보함으로써, 임상적, 상업적 협상에서 절대적 우위를 점했음을 시사합니다. FDMT의 가장 중요한 임상 3상 데이터(4D-150) 발표는 2027년 하반기로 예정되어 있습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ed60090b-dd2a-426f-909d-f332c03250eb" style="color:#444746;">4</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f84662fa-9d35-46b3-be2c-71ee0f88c6e9" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 대부분의 바이오텍은 이 '데이터 발표' 직전에 현금이 고갈되어, 불리한 조건으로 유상증자(소위 '관뚜껑 덮는 파이낸싱')를 하거나 빅파마에 헐값에 매각됩니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a9f9458e-615f-4c6d-8072-0564d8b33168" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4abf2ca6-65de-471c-aeeb-f1cfee0f4335" style="color:#1b1c1d;">FDMT는 2024년 2월(300M USD 증자)과 2025년 10월(85M USD + 50M USD 계약)을 통해 이 재무적 절벽(Financial Cliff)을 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-b74cb5ff-0b5f-48a9-9f24-661b6f689a8a" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>완전히 제거</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f117871b-10bb-44a1-a34a-a8a4c46020b7" style="color:#1b1c1d;">했습니다. 현재 보유한 현금(506M USD + 85M USD)과 R&amp;D 비용 절감(50M USD)은 2027년 H2 데이터 발표 이후, 2028년 BLA(신약 허가) 신청 및 상업화 준비까지의 모든 비용을 충당합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-95eb9d14-5c67-48ab-9ebe-07be2990c182" style="color:#444746;">4</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b02c7f73-a24b-4bef-99a4-3b7fc922c577" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 이는 FDMT가 향후 3년간 시장의 변동성이나 자금 조달 압박과 무관하게 오로지 '임상 성공'에만 집중할 수 있음을 의미하며, 이는 투자자에게 제공되는 가장 큰 안전 마진입니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ee8567f2-33b4-4833-a7f7-3465facfdf24" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-cee7710a-5f1d-4d82-905e-307fb96eb6fd" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-19bb592c-0d99-47fe-99c5-e9b759bcc16b" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs24 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3f658a8f-9b1c-41ee-b88d-a899e16688b4" style="color:#1b1c1d;">IV. 기업의 해자(Moat): 'Therapeutic Vector Evolution' 플랫폼</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ad18d7f2-fa6c-4231-b519-564db5e180ca" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-f72888ef-85ce-4dc8-9b4e-a09a35b5b3f9" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-69be00a0-8fc7-479b-ba72-8b739414cc49" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-a48b6a93-b8e4-43ac-93a4-501d7fcb6dd5" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-3a4d97e8-d4b9-4610-84b6-f55aebe1db99" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0fd0fe44-7beb-4cf7-b3dd-3472bccd2388" style="color:#1b1c1d;">가. 기술적 해자: 유전자 치료의 '배송 문제' 해결</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-1b6ed15f-8ea7-420f-9f52-7d3768bf21f1" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-5224b1d6-a6d4-4964-8ea1-979831c60353" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-0d6fa089-5253-45c5-8215-15c7eacf60cb" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6ff80fc8-66da-46a6-adbf-5ad6f7ce1de8" style="color:#1b1c1d;">유전자 치료의 성패는 '유전자' 자체가 아니라, 그 유전자를 원하는 세포에 '안전하고 효율적으로 배송'하는 벡터(AAV)에 달려있습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1874d2b8-53c6-4be1-b499-0148c3d01125" style="color:#444746;">10</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-bee6142d-1761-40e9-a96d-745d400578bf" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> FDMT의 해자는 '유도 진화'를 통해 약 10억 개의 합성 캡시드 라이브러리 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-59200a63-6f8d-4b86-8c01-7900c381d2e1" style="color:#444746;">11</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4fd5cbd3-53b7-4cf2-b4ee-8a19dd2866f6" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에서 특정 '목표 벡터 프로필(TVP)' </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c84cf004-fd9e-4102-80b4-740e2ac49e6b" style="color:#444746;">10</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b1744081-a939-4c61-86f7-cb174243764d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에 가장 적합한 벡터를 '발명'하는 능력입니다. 이는 경쟁사들이 사용하는 자연형 AAV(AAV2, AAV8 등) </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-cf2be925-affc-4afa-a6c7-cbec7abfff65" style="color:#444746;">33</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-7c650eaf-a80c-4a0c-a67b-2be419acac62" style="color:#1b1c1d;">와는 근본적으로 다릅니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-cb9f7dba-bb03-452a-86a4-3230d1639bf0" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-0bcd03fb-c4b3-497a-8e29-666b27cb4cf3" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-df3b3a1d-5a7f-4b6e-8eb4-130eb06829aa" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-131dc918-b383-4e17-989b-8a76b9dcab4b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">나. 해자의 산물: 맞춤형 벡터 포트폴리오</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-6cbe1f6e-39ba-4bf3-8e78-b0692cf84d6b" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-62d16497-9c2e-42ad-92f5-c512f472a4eb" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-19184b4a-21ea-40cd-85e1-c017c8ef5a25" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c55c453a-867d-4c8f-8001-aeed496d6196" style="color:#1b1c1d;">R100 (안과용):</span></p></li></ol><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-12e441b4-b5ca-46f4-99d1-e9238d4964de" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3ceff34c-d8be-4325-b57e-86e5afd94cd2" style="color:#1b1c1d;">기존 문제: 망막은 내부 경계막(ILM)이라는 물리적 장벽으로 보호됩니다. AAV2 같은 기존 벡터는 이를 통과하지 못해, 안구를 절개하고 망막 아래로 직접 주사하는 '망막하 수술(Subretinal Injection)'이 필요했습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-92b7548b-e7c9-4f42-8a2a-18a0d5f1d691" style="color:#444746;">34</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-c44d0053-ee93-4929-8263-6be60cbc2206" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ed1ca9e3-977b-4175-b835-db6e15465f59" style="color:#1b1c1d;">R100의 해자: R100은 ILM 장벽을 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-1a3a1989-ebc2-4164-bf49-0b0bb3f30aa4" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>뚫고 들어가도록</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-cb025629-258e-4858-be02-c5bd698796ae" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 진화되었습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f6976381-5e36-4ae4-b6e6-b0d8c32933f9" style="color:#444746;">21</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-4ce19adf-f38c-444b-8fc6-34ba481d66ef" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c87ef0ac-b0f9-47dd-8b65-b16d27ff562d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">임상적 의미: 수술 없이, 외래에서 간단히 시행하는 '유리체강 내 주사(IVT)'만으로 망막 전체에 유전자 전달이 가능합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d0b6b0d3-7e29-40e7-9133-ec3ac588ef11" style="color:#444746;">21</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-85bb167f-11d3-42dd-bc22-5585a3d6412b" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1be87e9c-5502-4584-94d3-9b255dfe1a3a" style="color:#1b1c1d;">데이터: 기존 AAV2 대비 최대 10배 우월한 망막 세포 투과(Transduction) 효율을 보입니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b6f06a0f-1987-4290-b6ba-ee700733c84f" style="color:#444746;">35</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-8bdd817c-7c1f-4d3b-aac2-63e5964801d9" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-500cc6a9-a886-48d7-8066-2f10b98e05b8" style="color:#1b1c1d;">R100의 '고효율'이 4D-150의 '안전성'을 담보한다는 점은 매우 중요합니다. 안구 내 유전자 치료의 가장 큰 부작용은 '염증(Inflammation)'이며, 이는 주로 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-dce044c6-82b6-4589-ad40-8ddf0426954e" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>높은 용량의 벡터</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e52ff577-2f6b-4769-8cb7-a134c487b897" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 투여로 인해 발생합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-092934ba-341e-4d78-90d8-0228d87b4146" style="color:#444746;">36</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-47f7fe5f-baee-44c1-a4c1-a9d8419c1be1" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 경쟁사 Adverum (ixo-vec)이 심각한 염증 및 실명 부작용으로 고전한 이유도 높은 용량 때문입니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-facd6035-d2f9-49dd-a94c-09060ae6fb02" style="color:#444746;">37</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-93db4a51-67b6-43ab-85ca-dd5aa3ed172e" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> R100은 10배 더 효율적이므로 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-948bad66-032f-4d6d-b844-3fae229bc11a" style="color:#444746;">35</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-db671e8d-ec0b-4987-9556-c18e3456e47f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, 10분의 1의 용량만으로도 동일하거나 더 나은 효과를 낼 수 있습니다. 4D-150은 실제로 경쟁사 대비 *낮은 용량(low dose)*을 사용하며 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-25c3754e-280d-43ce-b36e-fce3a72a37c1" style="color:#444746;">38</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-622e63f7-5145-47b6-9d3c-b5ed516c9ad0" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, 그 결과 3상 용량에서 "임상적으로 유의미한 염증이 보고되지 않았습니다".</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-cbf9fcd2-618c-4ae5-8223-f058cdeee1bd" style="color:#444746;">2</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1c830e01-2447-4cb2-84e1-c13e26c28981" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 즉, FDMT는 '효율' 문제를 해결함으로써 '안전' 문제를 선제적으로 회피했습니다.</span></p><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-afb232f5-3783-4fae-9028-70c7610f5f2a" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-69cd8eea-09cd-4f5e-8a6c-0fa1b6248844" style="color:#1b1c1d;">A101 (폐 전용):</span></p></li></ol><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-5b8378a3-6217-4f6a-84c0-69d9d45d801c" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ea04ecbd-47ba-4bfd-bb9d-5195a9ca130e" style="color:#1b1c1d;">기존 문제: 에어로졸(흡입) 방식으로 폐 상피세포에 AAV를 전달하는 것은 극도로 어려웠습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ac8aefc6-a6f3-46d8-86d5-1f98fcc1d2ee" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d6b945eb-a645-4bc8-b224-8c3daf8bdfb7" style="color:#1b1c1d;">A101의 해자: 에어로졸 투여 후 낭포성 섬유증(CF) 환자의 폐에서 CFTR 유전자 발현을 성공적으로 입증한 **'세계 최초의 AAV 벡터'**입니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-13f2124d-c1de-43d2-8ab7-f1f46bd23724" style="color:#444746;">40</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a759266c-7967-40cd-9c95-418972c0a8a0" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 이는 유전자 치료의 불모지였던 '폐 질환' 시장을 개척했음을 의미합니다.</span></p></li></ul><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-6fe0471b-e045-4958-bc44-6c90f977cdde" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-71d4de43-fd1c-4f86-bf1e-2f8a7ad0b278" style="color:#1b1c1d;">C102 (심장 전용): 정맥주사(IV)로 심장 근육에 특이적으로 유전자를 전달하도록 설계되었습니다.</span></p></li></ol><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-3521753f-3a3c-467e-ab3f-d244fd9579e0" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-798e4d41-1b75-4bbd-a2cf-9dc6574edd38" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-c9360241-cd2a-4f4c-8194-892473e6f848" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-671b5f72-0e1a-4c66-899a-ac35c1c03919" style="color:#1b1c1d;">다. 관계적/정치적 해자</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-74b2bd45-4f66-460b-8f9d-3e7d18f3d61e" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-3c03aa26-66da-4380-bb99-9691496a4b2a" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-5790e798-848b-4301-9025-8ec4b6704eca" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-892b768c-7140-49fb-8d4d-fe960dc1a6c9" style="color:#1b1c1d;">낭포성 섬유증 재단 (Cystic Fibrosis Foundation, CFF): CFF는 단순한 환자 단체가 아닌, Vertex의 신화를 만들어낸 막강한 자금력과 전문성을 갖춘 R&amp;D 기관입니다.</span></p></li></ol><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a67872f8-c28a-46a1-bef1-b999ca60beda" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-12c3d54f-911a-4eaf-b084-ac17d0492c79" style="color:#1b1c1d;">CFF는 4DMT의 A101 플랫폼의 데이터를 검토한 후, 4D-710 프로그램에 총 3,200만 달러(약 430억 원)에 달하는 자금을 지원했습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-828e3a44-a8b5-41a9-9f16-04724c8f93fa" style="color:#444746;">42</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a80a3d25-891d-4cb2-b709-41d4ca70125d" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f2bf08b8-e233-423d-9a7b-4674f7d74091" style="color:#1b1c1d;">이는 CFF의 '과학적 보증(Scientific Validation)'을 의미합니다. CFF는 4DMT와 '공동 조향 위원회(JSC)'를 설립하여 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e58a5e53-bba5-4624-b57f-8860bd8e513f" 해석됩니다.</span></p></li></ul><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-faa905b0-4c4d-4abe-9d56-5383ed12ee9f" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b6295a1d-342a-4f3d-bec8-851959b9db9a" style="color:#1b1c1d;">John F. Milligan, Ph.D. (이사회 의장, Executive Chairman):</span></p></li></ol><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-6a491361-98d6-40d0-9394-814b70076dad" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-89898e68-80eb-4af5-8a55-2e977d9c9c10" style="color:#1b1c1d;">프로필: Gilead Sciences의 전(前) CEO 겸 사장.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d41f435e-914d-4a8a-9ee3-9ccdece32b64" style="color:#444746;">9</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-5385f707-35db-4a95-ad9f-1741959d61a8" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-cb23cab9-44c9-4489-8789-3ff7c44eebc7" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Milligan 의장의 존재는 FDMT의 가장 강력한 '경영적 해자'입니다. 그는 Gilead를 HIV 및 C형 간염(Sovaldi 신화) 분야의 글로벌 거대 기업으로 성장시킨 주역입니다. 바이오 업계에서 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-4c74f3f5-be77-4964-bde2-182946699943" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>상업화, M&amp;A, 빅파마 협상</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-22aa7577-2a3a-46f6-b544-381087bee9ad" style="color:#1b1c1d;">의 최고 전문가 중 한 명인 그가 수많은 바이오텍 중 FDMT를 선택하여 단순한 사외이사가 아닌 '집행 의장(Executive Chairman)' </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-959ca728-c00e-46b8-a53a-c50432f62f8f" style="color:#444746;">9</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-12cd3aef-5292-4034-9948-35408cec6b38" style="color:#1b1c1d;">으로 합류했다는 사실 자체가 FDMT 플랫폼의 잠재력에 대한 강력한 보증입니다. 최근 Otsuka와의 APAC 딜 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-62379eed-1929-4238-885b-9f2fecc07b04" style="color:#444746;">5</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4dbe6cdd-071e-4d1e-8f29-643d1c7e6a0b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">이나 4D-150/4D-710 중심의 파이프라인 집중 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-503fd59d-6542-4a78-a9a2-e34c14e6c794" style="color:#444746;">4</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c899078e-df67-4b82-911b-d0533a6275cf" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 결정은, 막대한 현금 창출 자산에 집중하여 기업 가치를 극대화했던 Milligan의 'Gilead 전략'을 연상시킵니다.</span></p></li></ul><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-88521bf5-41a9-4f4a-a8b1-98a2383e60f0" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-2754f0d0-2a9f-44d9-856f-a2f85046e98d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">후기 임상 및 상업화 인력 확보:</span></p></li></ol><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-66204311-023e-42d8-999d-75662d43275e" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-5cee673e-964e-4601-992c-e754f79a9d41" style="color:#1b1c1d;">최근 FDMT는 BLA(신약 허가) 신청을 대비해 '후기 임상 개발 경험이 입증된 임원'들을 영입하고 있습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9e7b09b5-1ba9-4f06-8bfa-57e406560f9e" style="color:#444746;">60</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-20d7409a-e05c-4107-b9a5-c2894c79402e" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-436db6f2-ea9a-43a2-b3f4-c744cbc82729" style="color:#1b1c1d;">특히 주목할 만한 인물은 Wiley A. Chambers, M.D. </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-16ba1c6d-c1d5-45ec-80f5-ac5b442b05d5" style="color:#444746;">60</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4cd35f26-3656-449d-a08c-3542821edd8e" style="color:#1b1c1d;">입니다. 그는 FDA 안과 부문 총괄 디렉터(Director) 출신입니다. 4D-150의 BLA 승인을 받아내야 할 FDA의 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-2701b587-0662-4648-b6b2-36bc6cf8743e" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>전(前) 핵심 규제 책임자</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-26319856-173f-4fdd-86db-5365c0dcb213" style="color:#1b1c1d;">를 자문위원으로 영입한 것은, 규제 전략에 있어 '정답'을 알고 움직이겠다는 강력한 의지의 표명입니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-40de08c3-e112-4c3d-87e5-8c8016cc8790" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" style="color:#1b1c1d;">RA Capital Management:</span></p></li></ol><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-b089eaf8-2df6-4983-ac32-329662aae933" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3218d002-f920-4d62-97d2-ffbe4fa93a88" style="color:#1b1c1d;">성격: 하버드와 MIT의 수많은 PhD/MD 인력을 보유한, </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-8b41ec67-f339-4051-a1d9-0db7e6720683" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>과학적 증거 기반</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d7f2542f-02b1-46fc-baea-6b2c6142d242" style="color:#1b1c1d;">의 바이오 전문 투자사입니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-44f4da21-f7f0-4afb-b951-4175b0ff837a" style="color:#444746;">66</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-2da1bad6-e251-4201-b9b7-9c976b245403" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-70327752-867a-4a7b-a226-75d28c383efc" style="color:#1b1c1d;">투자 철학: 초기 Seed/Private 단계부터 IPO, 상장 후(Public)까지 장기 투자하는 멀티 스테이지 전략을 구사합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-cab79202-4c1e-47eb-aede-016e0454c189" style="color:#444746;">68</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-702d14f2-e675-4e02-990f-49c3e85a0ea9" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-51efb354-5df3-40be-868e-c64413462335" style="color:#1b1c1d;">시사점: RA Capital의 대규모 지분 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-bf9a5be2-e37d-4fab-95ba-f4b62c6a7a29" style="color:#444746;">64</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9b7720d3-405a-404e-a2ee-c52b33a8feed" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 보유는 4DMT의 'Therapeutic Vector Evolution' 플랫폼이 이들 전문가 집단의 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-ff0e4708-dfe7-43bd-9d69-fe39eed72cd8" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>엄격한 과학적 실사(Due Diligence)를 통과</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-30d3ea4f-26bf-44c9-a30b-1b2ac7dbca6e" style="color:#1b1c1d;">했음을 의미합니다. 이들은 단기 트레이더가 아닌 '동반자(Company Builder)'입니다.</span></p></li></ul><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a7208006-265f-4d90-838e-125ed5387fb9" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ca3710ef-a7ae-41c9-b4f8-887721d747dc" style="color:#1b1c1d;">BVF Partners (Biotechnology Value Fund):</span></p></li></ol><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-94900fe4-baba-49f8-b6e1-f83e5e992142" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-baaf9552-b1bd-4331-afd3-ea712146bd4a" style="color:#1b1c1d;">성격: 1993년 설립된, </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-8cf1d376-55ce-44de-b960-910b76bc4cb1" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>소형주 및 중소형주 바이오 기업</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e134267b-76ed-4da5-9707-23569f328726" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에 집중하는 펀더멘털 기반의 헤지펀드입니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6cc35198-2365-4185-97d2-56dc17897726" style="color:#444746;">69</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-f95b7afe-bcab-4f4a-aca6-6304c80982b3" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b6979224-4714-499e-9c7c-9332f66bfc37" style="color:#1b1c1d;">투자 철학: </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-3dc037d5-206a-47dc-8632-938d5726448e" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>장기적인 관점</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a1d66f5e-aff5-45ec-b359-5b243ad6cfbf" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에서 소수의 포트폴리오(25-30개)에 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-a75a2978-6fed-41de-8fe6-3d5392da920b" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>집중(Concentrated)</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-610d36b8-7506-4fa9-a6f6-032b2c9028f5" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 투자합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f19250a8-499e-4527-bd81-9bfdc430e55d" style="color:#444746;">71</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ce24118c-41e7-41c2-9042-cd78f0782613" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 단기적인 시장 변동성으로 인해 저평가된, '비대칭적 위험-보상(Asymmetric Risk-Reward)'을 가진 기업을 발굴합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ef793f59-f7b0-475e-9b60-48ca274c4b20" style="color:#444746;">71</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-701a5566-fc4e-4238-85d6-c959a57c09b0" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-39583c91-b7a2-4d8f-92af-277b9cdeaeb0" style="color:#1b1c1d;">시사점: BVF가 Series C </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1a8b256d-be5c-42b4-a40d-b3d8aa7ea346" style="color:#444746;">48</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-8e6046af-6a66-4014-983f-99d7979f39c6" style="color:#1b1c1d;">부터 현재까지 대규모 지분을 유지 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-fbd7a2c1-7cb9-4718-8226-00d462d383f6" style="color:#444746;">64</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-db2ac88d-5890-4dc4-ad29-62357611086d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">하는 것은, 4DMT의 현재 주가가 회사의 근본적인 플랫폼 가치와 4D-150의 잠재력 대비 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-08eb299b-1acc-4938-b1b8-679f76807d3f" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>심각하게 저평가</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9dee28ec-dea9-4f73-943c-ba65697363e6" style="color:#1b1c1d;">되어 있다고 판단하고 있음을 시사합니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-8a46d32d-b276-43d1-ba49-ab2461976342" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0d62e768-73e2-4f95-9e01-8f1b85be2cd5" style="color:#1b1c1d;">결론적으로, FDMT의 주주 구성은 '일반 투자자'가 아닌 '전문가(Specialists)'에 의해 주도되고 있습니다. 상위 주주 리스트(BVF, RA Capital, Novo Holdings, Viking)는 미국 바이오 투자 업계의 '스마트 머니'로 불리는 최고 전문가 집단입니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9c383257-f6b5-4749-b208-55d992b3c6da" style="color:#444746;">63</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ddf42c79-efe5-43d6-9ce9-b18a1e1c2a71" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 이들은 R100 벡터가 AAV2와 어떻게 다른지, Adverum의 염증 데이터가 무엇을 의미하는지 정확히 이해하고 있습니다. 또한 이들은 2027년 H2 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-5e67bafe-8e63-4316-a714-5ece014ddf8e" style="color:#444746;">4</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6035e791-6bb4-4ab0-8d7d-f04ad5aa1369" style="color:#1b1c1d;">라는 장기적인 임상 일정을 인내할 수 있는 자본(Patient Capital)을 보유하고 있습니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-13d7b48e-bf98-4bdc-b82a-068d64c4eef1" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-be39b6be-e264-4f26-a99e-19f6963f5aa7" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-443e51f7-27b6-4969-bb66-899084d65caa" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs24 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3ecc93ec-a99c-4bd2-bfcb-15a900a4195e" style="color:#1b1c1d;">VII. 경쟁 환경 분석</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-50708ca1-572d-4dd3-9fe4-0d4b07f3bff8" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-ce18a9fe-b252-4471-baa6-f8d1de31324a" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-bbb27cb9-365f-4d7f-9180-afd73578c6a1" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-a2314708-76fe-490a-9705-1c5c90d31774" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-3035d5aa-7133-4b0a-b0a6-beacbc42120d" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c2f53141-c2d0-4b53-9b69-380ef9732976" style="color:#1b1c1d;">가. 4DMT의 핵심 경쟁 우위: '플랫폼'이 '제품'의 경쟁력을 결정한다</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-0fea1417-c1cc-41ea-a8dc-bb8d8b9353f0" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-f10df252-e5aa-4ace-aa4d-5d68b6797de1" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-978cbe3c-db67-4a5f-bff8-8f1c47957b38" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-20a24e0a-90ef-4dea-939c-9fb4a4def47b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">4DMT의 전략은 경쟁사와 *동일한(혹은 더 나은) 유전자(Payload)*를, *압도적으로 우월한 벡터(Delivery)*로 전달하는 것입니다. 4D-150은 시장에서 검증된 aflibercept (Eylea) 성분과 anti-VEGF-C RNAi를 R100 벡터로 전달합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-91e1e12f-7fe5-4985-b129-888ebb4055c2" style="color:#444746;">72</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b33131d9-e311-40aa-8720-251d5b378317" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 경쟁력은 R100의 안전성과 편의성에서 나옵니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-6fa2f129-6e40-4f0a-b031-eed2be13da1d" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-430cd49f-dd94-4fcc-b8fc-2a44c1f5f7bc" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-948206b0-0e8d-477d-b51b-581a9aee3645" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6c8ad6a1-f10f-4e2b-a8d6-e81dca0dc75b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">나. 안과 (Wet AMD): 4D-150의 '골디락스(Goldilocks)' 포지셔닝</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-f1bbd091-fc9c-415d-8ce0-cf8014cdf1fe" style=""><span
