<div class="se-module se-module-text">
<!-- SE-TEXT { --><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-70f06f35-7e8d-4be2-9e76-88cb06a9fecc" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-58c8b177-c411-47e2-a4f2-6a4b434a9f5f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">vs. REGENXBIO/AbbVie (RGNX): '편의성'의 승리</span></p></li></ol><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-9f0fa469-cc2b-4011-8981-1fadbabaa658" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-38fa4e31-750f-4976-8504-125f64b98502" style="color:#1b1c1d;">RGNX는 3상 임상에서 '망막하 수술(SR)' 방식을 사용합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c44e1754-c430-491f-8878-9a97571e9cab" style="color:#444746;">3</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0570b3ac-becf-4ce5-ab7b-01552aae936d" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 이는 의사에게는 수술 부담, 환자에게는 수술 위험, 보험사에게는 높은 비용을 의미합니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-0004b501-6fc3-45de-963b-5fbf979b6e00" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f88efec5-a198-4229-8eaa-414c185ab0a3" style="color:#1b1c1d;">RGNX도 이 약점을 인지하고, 뒤늦게 '맥락막상(SC)' 주사 방식을 2상에서 테스트하고 있습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0b457882-8ccd-43bd-aa0c-4ee052266b56" style="color:#444746;">3</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-3090315f-708e-49a1-b30e-4a86327d012d" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-55b9fe1b-effb-480b-8947-f105e8754fd4" style="color:#1b1c1d;">결론: 4D-150은 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-3b2397dc-8a0a-4817-b18e-06b72b9f53b7" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>현재 3상에서</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-635ebb78-5c76-4ff2-a36f-a3113f89319e" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 가장 간단하고 보편적인 'IVT 주사' 방식 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-7e4b2965-c009-409f-aa2d-045f7ff569a1" style="color:#444746;">1</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3b0ec00d-b479-4948-a488-28bd95c934ae" style="color:#1b1c1d;">을 사용합니다. 이는 시장 출시 시, 전체 안과 의사(약 2만 명)가 즉시 시술할 수 있음을 의미하며, 이는 SR 수술이 가능한 극소수의 망막 전문의만 대상으로 하는 RGNX 대비 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-86fa2452-fc76-4a63-98bd-b287d673bf93" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>상업적 침투율에서 비교가 불가능</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c5333dcd-94fd-4ed8-ae96-9d703d38b693" style="color:#1b1c1d;">합니다.</span></p></li></ul><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-8c073c54-0f35-457c-9623-f49d406c5c3c" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3f527e8f-20e9-4107-901b-533090b7b84b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">vs. Adverum (ADVM): '안전성'의 승리</span></p></li></ol><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-9521c4c5-8d4a-4712-bdb4-24130770fb3e" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-61472ecd-0f6b-4511-9c20-d9a15384bf00" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Adverum은 FDMT와 동일한 IVT 주사 방식을 시도했으나, </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-43849c8f-036d-43a0-85aa-849d97c0d896" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>치명적인 안전성 문제</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-581138b2-15a1-4c2d-95c5-eb8d3998fdfa" style="color:#1b1c1d;">에 부딪혔습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-8cb60cf2-a3c6-4749-b968-236ac3493b3f" style="color:#444746;">74</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-20a9e640-419c-4f15-9dd6-51e12173c121" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d199f2ed-cdce-4531-8b7a-3e23f5b171b4" style="color:#1b1c1d;">높은 용량의 벡터 투여로 인해 심각한 안구 내 염증(IOI)이 발생했고, 당뇨병성 황반 부종(DME) 임상에서는 환자가 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-a917c09d-b5c3-4aaf-895a-d9e0cf3a4ac3" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>실명</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ba9b4ac1-9a05-4a64-b6cc-c9a870e9507e" style="color:#1b1c1d;">하는 사태까지 발생하여 해당 임상이 중단되었습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d2da5c61-a527-41c3-8cd8-8daae17c23ff" style="color:#444746;">37</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-5dfdb6a7-dd48-42c2-bca0-68d4ee158ce2" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c3d745be-9fc4-4060-b98f-070cfc58ec8e" style="color:#1b1c1d;">결론: 4D-150은 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-a2ac42dc-9976-43b9-a1a4-9d36f0ff389e" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>더 효율적인 R100 벡터</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-406697b3-fd4b-4260-b0ab-2a60972ce129" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 덕분에 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-41795f31-e873-4496-8955-4fa9af96b417" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>더 낮은 용량</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-fa1a4eb6-d7fa-4c97-b210-25c2547edaf1" style="color:#1b1c1d;">을 사용, Adverum이 실패한 '안전한 IVT 주사'를 유일하게 성공시켰습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4f73982d-23f9-4ee2-a7df-696206920fe8" style="color:#444746;">38</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1d2764ee-270d-496c-9143-6cf5647aa99c" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 139명의 환자 데이터에서 3상 용량 기준 "유의미한 염증"이 없었다는 데이터 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-406b9da8-7901-4ab1-9fc4-d95cd67d1dfc" style="color:#444746;">2</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-26201eb5-0755-4af5-9ae4-86eebff7d9df" style="color:#1b1c1d;">는 FDMT의 가장 강력한 경쟁 우위입니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ee14dd9c-55c0-4879-8fa4-48d6a52698c5" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-6553d507-68e1-4430-9d36-e6e7f3f6f085" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a3da5c1d-d85b-414f-86b5-cca830049092" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-82267cbf-d0dd-465b-b77f-c838bbe70e68" style="color:#1b1c1d;">다. 폐 (Cystic Fibrosis): 4D-710 vs. Vertex</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ddc28231-75e7-4116-934a-8dfb18ae4c7f" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-bcd136bb-9b0d-4118-aaf9-db718ce191f7" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-6af00434-0b5a-4e67-8b40-017edbe82b3b" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6ea147ae-0f1a-4de1-b6bd-08ca83ece9e2" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Vertex (현재 SoC): CFTR 조절제(Modulator) 약물로 시장을 독점하고 있습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-7983ddcc-01c0-4b15-ade5-9049562b1fd6" style="color:#444746;">16</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a3049a37-c6b8-48ba-8b1b-2c5f796dfa5f" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d7db1035-c72d-47be-93e0-39978819f2d2" style="color:#1b1c1d;">FDMT의 틈새시장: Vertex의 약물은 특정 유전자 변이(e.g., F508del)에만 작용하며, *전체 환자의 약 10%*는 이러한 약물이 듣지 않는 변이를 가지고 있습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-f0c6dca0-f59b-46f5-ad5f-3aaeb72c7a80" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-02df7e69-48f0-4ccd-9c7f-9195e4052501" style="color:#1b1c1d;">4D-710의 전략: Vertex의 약물이 듣지 않는 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-a8022de6-80b4-47ac-8170-5a87754ee214" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>모든</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d0a48440-1f40-4bb1-b499-f0225d996212" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 환자에게 적용 가능한 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-a1ed9038-a236-4aad-b953-d35b9702a060" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>근본적 유전자 치료제</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-850a0367-d413-42e9-a216-642bbc3d73ae" style="color:#1b1c1d;">로 포지셔닝합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-fbbe28ed-8885-4b13-a803-fe63a4b1ee71" style="color:#444746;">16</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-302fcda0-4040-47f6-aa5b-f0605cddc72b" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> A101 벡터를 통해 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-e1a76cec-50f8-424d-9a6b-063054a8b4a2" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>정상 CFTR 유전자</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-84425873-4a15-48bb-a86a-2628aa30bed8" style="color:#1b1c1d;">를 폐에 직접 전달합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6733f3ff-231d-4a22-9e76-fad6b8083950" style="color:#444746;">42</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-7144f732-1797-4b50-8000-e177865ad867" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a1146202-b153-43eb-9bfe-e39ddd9aacc0" style="color:#1b1c1d;">경쟁 우위: 4DMT는 CFF의 32M USD 지원 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a15018ae-57d1-4a08-90ba-77ba93ec7619" style="color:#444746;">42</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c516d945-35a8-4a92-a68d-0b2f0bdf3099" style="color:#1b1c1d;">을 업고, 이 분야에서 가장 앞선 '흡입형 AAV 유전자 치료제' 개발사입니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-b7265f44-3110-4ef4-86d9-b8822baaad0b" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-7583f699-52cd-4b05-9804-e343388ffb72" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-5a05bc4a-0cae-46c3-a0b3-0741ab87fd05" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs24 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-7f9ac555-a19a-462e-8a8d-351b4016caf9" style="color:#1b1c1d;">VIII. 성장 전략 및 리스크 분석</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-52673d50-9aaf-4b47-a34f-724ab786541f" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-1bd348c3-cab3-487b-829e-40b07e753dfd" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-6cff43a0-06c2-4ed6-9bb6-f77a495f3d5b" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-f508c3df-3ac2-459f-a320-4a306a558c82" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-c0ba0608-1ebf-4e67-96c0-f1af5e599ef9" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d433a8e0-2d75-4605-bb11-28b6638fe4fc" style="color:#1b1c1d;">가. 최근 8개 분기 R&amp;D 투자 전략: '선택과 집중'</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-de4aa1a6-0ba7-4d85-834a-46093df78d0f" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-46a5260d-d818-46ab-a998-898adabde369" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-2bc97c62-ac9e-405e-a3ca-746779d8d0f6" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-57e12994-d377-49b8-a691-6c595bd782c7" style="color:#1b1c1d;">FDMT는 2025년 1월, 파이프라인을 **4D-150(안과)**과 4D-710(폐) 두 개의 핵심 자산에 집중하기로 결정했습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-04a84128-b764-4679-924b-f4ef3aaeb4f5" style="color:#444746;">4</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b279ce3b-5242-4ecc-8e9c-e301701ef33d" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> Fabry 병(4D-310) 등 긍정적 데이터 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-7aabeb11-5515-4ace-9d61-24fa43c7f526" style="color:#444746;">51</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-17672b8b-f235-4690-adf7-f88ed8e34811" style="color:#1b1c1d;">가 나오던 프로그램조차 전략적 우선순위에서 조정하며, 모든 자원을 두 개의 '블록버스터 후보'에 쏟아붓고 있습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ef95588d-c34f-46fe-8f80-4ebb178236be" style="color:#444746;">76</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-2e172109-4a9c-4f55-9acb-b825f67489da" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-b436a8a0-0b8a-4d0a-aded-2e55b8f33da0" style="color:#1b1c1d;">이는 전형적인 'Gilead 스타일'의 자원 배분입니다. '플랫폼 기업'의 함정(여러 개의 소규모 파이프라인에 자원을 분산하는 것)에서 벗어나, 가장 확실하고(Strongest clinical proof of concept </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-2b34a70c-ecef-420c-a838-cf079036ea70" style="color:#444746;">4</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3960ce18-e6d0-4c78-b773-92b2bbc22cf6" style="color:#1b1c1d;">) 가장 시장이 큰(Large market </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-8ddd05fc-dc35-4163-a23f-59ae36638556" style="color:#444746;">1</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e0101471-b82d-4bdf-8b50-1876ea3c80cb" style="color:#1b1c1d;">) 자산에 '올인'하는, 성숙한 경영진의 전략적 판단입니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-d0ce0fd9-7ee4-4a48-a776-4b8e209041cf" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-bc1a0a90-ffed-4205-8438-d2dbb330bdef" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-7198a1ed-7b95-4d2c-83e8-585bac92c8f5" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9d15ff1a-2564-4fb8-94c7-24717183b070" style="color:#1b1c1d;">나. 비희석성(Non-Dilutive) 자금 조달 전략</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-96ad53de-76d4-4d9d-b248-6e491a766503" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-05420d8b-a633-4147-8180-1dd8220da8f5" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-11805545-76a4-4b33-a694-4d8f296251de" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4d6a366e-0baa-418c-87e0-e2ce17ce86cd" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Otsuka와의 4D-150 APAC 라이선스 계약 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e0211171-c6ec-4c23-8753-9e3d49504205" style="color:#444746;">5</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-8ace13ed-75a6-4d78-8bbc-6a80be4b3f9f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">은 성장 전략의 핵심입니다.</span></p><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-795f841c-f1ea-4371-8d35-41de55c9feec" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-35b9b939-8a1e-46f0-aa50-91fe480880a7" style="color:#1b1c1d;">현금 확보: 85M USD의 즉각적인 현금 유입.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-c5bb68a8-2914-493b-b251-9b92d1c0e2fc" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1b23d5f6-9dd1-49f9-a125-6ad853d8f0f6" style="color:#1b1c1d;">R&amp;D 비용 절감: 향후 3년간 50M USD 이상의 글로벌 3상 비용(FDMT가 지출해야 할 돈)을 Otsuka가 분담.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ead08f57-0cf1-476b-b3ad-3341757c4ba4" style="color:#444746;">5</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-28ca113d-ba9b-4235-8e28-e4281c3d1be5" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 이는 OCF burn rate를 극적으로 낮춥니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-ff38d6f9-ac62-46d0-a160-2c1326567889" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d76b1109-1cb6-41ad-8293-604896cc1518" style="color:#1b1c1d;">시장 검증: 4D-150 자산 가치에 대한 빅파마의 공식적인 가치 평가(Valuation)를 받았습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-1376707d-42a2-4853-9c0c-4b26c1165769" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-5bc8c9c4-a714-4220-8772-bf99f6f838b5" style="color:#1b1c1d;">지역 리스크 분산: FDMT는 가장 잘 할 수 있는 미국/유럽 시장에 집중하고, 복잡한 아시아 시장은 파트너(Otsuka)에게 맡겨 상업화 리스크를 분산시킵니다.</span></p></li></ol><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-8bb4215a-207b-4ad8-8532-4b996beac307" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-dfdbe392-2d3b-4b73-9b35-e99b7ba0b9bb" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-10b696f9-c521-4338-a323-cbbe324bdb57" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e4eae9ec-e432-4edb-9adb-c471c12f6435" style="color:#1b1c1d;">다. 잠재적 리스크: 오버행(Overhang) 및 기타 고려사항</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a62b9668-3c8f-4ab5-bd24-ed60e17c6d73" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-9e97da62-75ef-4809-921d-e80dd3e59ed6" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-cfc8f901-0046-4f48-ad40-3ede9fe18b83" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1524b8b5-a1a5-4eff-aaa9-1b4c9589673f" style="color:#1b1c1d;">전환사채(CB)/교환사채(EB): 현재 FDMT의 재무제표 상 주목할 만한 전환사채나 교환사채는 확인되지 않습니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-2b8adeae-0ffe-4c29-bb5e-716c3caf9689" style="color:#444746;">77</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9348b7e3-99fc-4014-ab68-77346378a0bd" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 부채 리스크는 매우 낮습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-9978bb63-adf6-4403-b87a-0748d7a3a381" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f9055462-f799-496c-aa28-5baadc746741" style="color:#1b1c1d;">잠재적 주식 물량 (Equity Overhang): 이것이 핵심 리스크입니다.</span></p></li></ol><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-dcdb9bdc-1e38-4802-8a26-1a4bd61350e5" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-924f0ad3-c70b-4c07-91bc-3969a046c37a" style="color:#1b1c1d;">사전 조달 신주인수권 (Pre-funded Warrants): 2025년 6월 30일 기준, 총 9,385,000주의 워런트가 존재합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6509f54e-9233-4a36-9139-546e87b50bc1" style="color:#444746;">7</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-2a7ae54d-2155-439a-928b-488535798ef9" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 이 워런트의 행사가는 0.0001 USD로 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c477c733-499d-405d-8caa-0f04b7df0ee4" style="color:#444746;">7</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6b9992bd-7a15-4672-b39c-c8cc3b4502f7" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, 사실상 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-85e4615e-9130-4f25-8e04-452bdd0b936f" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>즉시 행사가능한 주식</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e9c40ed2-46a3-472a-b7a4-f83266caac30" style="color:#1b1c1d;">과 동일합니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-661cb840-7241-4b6a-bca9-c73849af8c67" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-43082531-0199-4103-b6e5-ac72e94da0a0" style="color:#1b1c1d;">스톡옵션 (Stock Options): 2025년 3월 31일 기준, 총 4,853,646주의 스톡옵션이 존재하며, 가중 평균 행사가는 24.15 USD입니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9895ba15-6e8c-4b9b-ac95-37fced11dbfb" style="color:#444746;">8</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-0de14268-adb8-4f80-b7ee-2842e6217864" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9ae93df4-2a04-4faa-a5ab-22a954a1360b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">리스크 분석: 워런트 물량은 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-c05379ce-f3f2-448d-aa48-b64b4dbd4d61" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>미래의 위협</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a8e01a5e-a536-40c8-bb46-c8d457ed0e5e" style="color:#1b1c1d;">이 아니라 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-58da5b2c-3f76-4e79-8ad0-8fb3cab6948a" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>현재의 현실</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ea3a9742-5eb7-445d-aa77-43fa9e527efc" style="color:#1b1c1d;">입니다. 938만 주의 워런트 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ca463c7d-cf14-45db-996c-25eda5c33b64" style="color:#444746;">7</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-70d1c7ba-d6d0-40dd-8fd6-34f42bcedcbf" style="color:#1b1c1d;">는 2024년 2월 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d2374a35-41c1-4670-a4ed-e0ce6a98f5a3" style="color:#444746;">6</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-5046e807-9d05-4a19-a4e5-2030850fb81d" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 및 11월 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a5f80dcf-6a00-437c-b2ff-83e047499946" style="color:#444746;">7</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-8974e6ca-b210-4e42-96b2-7a6782300f6a" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 증자 시, BVF나 RA Capital 같은 대형 펀드들이 지분율 10% 신고 의무를 피하기 위해 주식 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-a3ae953c-f4b6-4449-b27c-f1564cd137dd" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>대신</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-d8d18356-84ed-4020-9916-8d99e5f21499" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 받은 것입니다. 시장의 전문 투자자들은 이미 이 물량을 총 발행 주식 수에 포함하여(Fully-diluted) 기업 가치를 계산하고 있습니다. 따라서 이 물량이 시장에 풀리는 것은 '새로운 악재'가 아니라, 이미 알려진 사실의 '기술적 이행'입니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-95bc0e61-2eff-4631-9630-bd285d68e289" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c5ca6948-df76-4958-bc96-397c89cb0e7b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">반면, 24.15 USD의 행사가를 가진 485만 주의 스톡옵션 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e39d595f-9ab0-4619-822c-f071c836f18d" style="color:#444746;">8</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c51ba33f-7dfe-434e-9fc4-0ae3f88f9e4a" style="color:#1b1c1d;">은 주가가 24.15 USD를 넘어야만(현재 주가 10.51 USD </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6eaf026c-a67d-47b3-aec3-0c7288b5911e" style="color:#444746;">62</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e6bd668d-eb91-44df-8470-6676b9abc004" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 대비 130% 이상 상승) 의미가 있습니다. 이는 주가 상승 시에만 발생하는 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-cf7587b2-227c-4cdd-aa98-e934ad23f6b2" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>성공에 따른 희석</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-2d451b7c-8ca3-46ba-8832-d4a41ac91aff" style="color:#1b1c1d;">이므로, 현재 주가 수준에서는 큰 위협이 아닙니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-180d8912-400b-4593-a1b9-91dac82517c2" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-7d10f4ca-d307-4c36-9a9c-71b2186b8653" style="color:#1b1c1d;">2028년까지의 현금 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-e05221ad-8415-4937-8c54-9a242d588ab9" style="color:#444746;">4</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6bf0b174-3dc7-436f-979f-c96febf31a45" style="color:#1b1c1d;">과 Otsuka/Astellas의 비희석성 자금 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c178376a-439a-4cc5-a651-fb4499d29b62" style="color:#444746;">5</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-58eca0b7-8e9b-4491-82c1-b8cc679fe247" style="color:#1b1c1d;">을 고려할 때, FDMT가 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-c9cbd74f-b52d-4a59-8ae6-3ec86e4f59f8" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>예상치 못한 대규모 저가 유상증자</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 style="color:#1b1c1d;">결론: 이러한 규제 환경은 '안전한 플랫폼'을 가진 기업에게는 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-e3a2297e-5e54-4035-80d4-23679c108e47" FDA의 강화된 안전성 기준은 4DMT의 '저용량 고효율' R100 벡터 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-3b848d50-0440-4574-9888-1e6ac2e6aa9c" id="SE-3e16efed-fa52-4deb-8217-eb6b5dbc086b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">로 상업적 허들을 완벽히 제거했습니다.</span></p></li></ul><ol class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-decimal"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-d6bb4fec-f3b8-4df0-a7f0-8aa92a1b0b0f" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f0195c83-82fd-4ccb-b838-88e63356271d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">명확한 핵심 촉매 (Clear Catalyst): 향후 3년의 로드맵이 명확합니다. 4FRONT-1, 4FRONT-2 (글로벌 3상)가 2025년 1분기에 시작되었으며 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6d245cbe-2458-4637-9cfd-7aeeacd61103" style="color:#444746;">4</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a4af8ea7-2b65-4249-8bba-8986325c4720" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, 최종 52주 데이터는 2027년 하반기에 발표될 예정입니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-27c48c5a-59e6-4220-ac73-701c2e90899d" style="color:#444746;">4</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-2135d2db-30b1-4f92-82cd-9b1198c8bd6c" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 투자자들은 이 명확한 이벤트를 기다리고 있습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-8bf28279-ba99-4bea-8250-d8eb280f860b" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-739dbed7-1b56-4d98-a70e-818477c90ed9" style="color:#1b1c1d;">상업적 가치 검증 (Commercial Validation): Otsuka가 85M USD의 계약금과 50M USD의 R&amp;D 비용을 지불 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-54799a3b-d9ac-4ae7-966a-d55d91819eeb" style="color:#444746;">5</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6ddd223e-8c25-49c3-87df-c125b2f42dca" style="color:#1b1c1d;">한 것은, 4D-150이 임상 3상에 실패할 리스크보다 </span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-7ac215ba-8f03-4e72-b12b-6c89051eab64" style="color:#1b1c1d;"><i>성공할 가능성에 훨씬 더 크게 베팅</i></span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-7c679d8d-ff56-4ac3-bb58-2be14df56ebb" style="color:#1b1c1d;">한 것입니다. 이는 자산 가치를 객관적으로 입증한 강력한 증거입니다.</span></p></li></ol><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-17d74817-b410-4bbc-9c85-e5cf77bb0cb7" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-26c163fe-59fa-4002-95ea-e8619863b55e" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-aad055e5-60a6-4229-9fa7-7743cdd26483" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs19 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-c122a865-6ad0-4999-ba32-a5864d49bd19" style="color:#1b1c1d;">라. 차순위(Runner-up) 분석: 4D-710 (낭포성 섬유증 치료제)</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-2a2416f5-646b-459a-b8c8-e5f1db1a3876" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-4de62733-4f5f-411b-a6ce-040ee357f351" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><ul class="se-text-list se-text-list-type-bullet-disc"><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-273ab7e1-cc4e-435e-889f-476ad076a633" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-75c52dc4-10c4-4711-87d0-0fa572d6ff0a" style="color:#1b1c1d;">잠재력: A101 벡터 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9fcec21c-995c-4d84-a1a4-d694eb2ff777" style="color:#444746;">41</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-9e3d12d5-95d2-452f-bd3a-9acdfafb1918" style="color:#1b1c1d;">는 유전자 치료의 불모지인 폐 질환을 정복할 잠재력을 가졌으며, CFF의 32M USD 지원 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-602d9631-0fa1-46cc-ad02-e7a316ff6a09" style="color:#444746;">42</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f18ed168-db66-4a08-8796-9d624e4c1ba5" style="color:#1b1c1d;">은 이 자산의 과학적 가치를 보증합니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-1d8974b9-8b9a-4309-a959-483fa664719d" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-5bfed41b-e8d4-4447-a33e-bff31f460339" style="color:#1b1c1d;">선정 보류 이유: 1) 4D-150보다 임상 단계가 빠릅니다 (이제 2상 진입).</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-ab2df7f9-210c-4d54-ba9d-f5d0b7e7affd" style="color:#444746;">42</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6dd6d6ca-552a-49aa-acf7-08ee0d5b4e7c" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 2) Vertex라는 강력한 시장 독점자가 존재합니다.</span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f7e8568e-371b-4fb4-89c7-9e7237df2c4d" style="color:#444746;">16</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-783a5164-96e8-4950-b01d-5c8013432502" style="color:#1b1c1d;"> 3) 향후 3년(2025-2027) 내에 3상 데이터와 같은 결정적인 가치 변곡점에 도달하기 어렵습니다.</span></p></li><li class="se-text-list-item"><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-d8dfebe5-7e1d-4965-94ee-482ae6ae66b9" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-f9014b5a-1ed2-43b8-a31a-7b9478476ddc" style="color:#1b1c1d;">투자 전략: 4D-710은 4D-150의 성공을 전제로 한 '장기 콜옵션(Long-term Call Option)'입니다. 4D-150이 2027년 성공하면, 그로 인한 재무적 여력은 4D-710을 비롯한 차세대 파이프라인의 가치를 극대화하는 데 사용될 것입니다.</span></p></li></ul><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-b5d22e75-a9f6-4980-99db-c733ee28348d" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-7f68b7ae-3334-42cb-b992-2cb62eb4faf1" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a9371eff-7814-47f7-9c6c-decbb07206dc" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs24 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-1b821b76-109a-48be-bfe7-ed6ab9a5e4f6" style="color:#1b1c1d;">X. 결론 및 최종 제언</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-5209e1cf-0e01-43f1-9116-b79b8d58bc51" style=""><span class="se-fs- se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset" id="SE-cf1fda4e-cad0-4d62-87ff-66640c8a8e86" style="color:#000000;">​</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-a3721225-a7ed-4a74-a74c-e66de2ded3bb" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-01615d18-cfef-4fe1-b44d-db55b3a5c605" style="color:#1b1c1d;">4D Molecular Therapeutics는 '플랫폼'의 잠재력을 '제품'의 경쟁력으로 성공적으로 치환하고 있는, 유전자 치료 2.0 섹터의 최선호주(Top Pick)입니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-4c77b3c4-e5c1-4898-a794-b469867cb804" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a1852bb0-7609-4e14-8772-71786983b32c" style="color:#1b1c1d;">동사의 핵심 해자인 'R100' 벡터는 170억 달러 규모의 습성 황반변성 시장에서 경쟁사들이 해결하지 못한 '안전성(vs. Adverum)'과 '편의성(vs. REGENXBIO)'이라는 두 가지 핵심 상업화 허들을 모두 해결했습니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-c7ff7f8d-8da5-4275-98b4-3edf72fe97fc" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-be6c1be3-1489-4eec-9c18-cf7ffbe864b3" style="color:#1b1c1d;">'탑픽'으로 선정한 4D-150은 2027년 하반기 3상 데이터 발표라는 명확한 이정표를 가지고 있으며, 회사는 Otsuka와의 파트너십과 두 차례의 대규모 자본 조달을 통해 2028년까지의 모든 재무적 리스크를 제거했습니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-da51331b-8856-4548-b187-b8fab51541dc" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-5f2ac1db-c186-4dc1-b92a-2cf6ddd83754" style="color:#1b1c1d;">Gilead의 전 CEO(John Milligan)가 이끄는 경영진 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-00d63f92-6678-4846-aaf0-b1cf63b22265" style="color:#444746;">9</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-0b2c7ab8-5659-46ae-a0ff-28641adbbb5d" style="color:#1b1c1d;">과 RA Capital, BVF 등 '스마트 머니' </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-47011f5c-5259-49d8-a10d-ee62672cd29a" style="color:#444746;">64</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-505beb91-1e05-4ee4-ab83-4438d4ac270c" style="color:#1b1c1d;">가 뒷받침하는 주주 구성은 이 회사의 전략적 의사결정에 대한 높은 신뢰를 부여합니다.</span></p><p class="se-text-paragraph se-text-paragraph-align-" id="SE-48ddc267-5cd7-4157-860d-e79571774f55" style=""><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-aa5f16e0-f73d-40bd-a803-9900957a08c6" style="color:#1b1c1d;">투자에 대한 핵심 리스크는 4D-150의 3상 실패이며, 이는 바이오텍 투자의 본질적 위험입니다. 그러나 4DMT는 현재까지 축적된 1/2상 데이터 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-6e8e9369-188b-47cd-908f-7eeb1008d266" style="color:#444746;">2</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-122b03ea-c270-4ff4-9712-de2ab46273e6" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, 차별화된 벡터 기술 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-4d5f2da9-5a12-4669-8d1a-87dd2eb132af" style="color:#444746;">35</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-a30403b2-e370-4bc5-b9b2-9ef6b36ce2ac" style="color:#1b1c1d;">, 그리고 경쟁사들의 연이은 실패 </span><span class="se-fs-fs11 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-732f0b18-9f43-4321-8005-a66762058cd8" style="color:#444746;">37</span><span class="se-fs-fs15 se-ff-nanumbareunhipi se-weight-unset se-style-unset" id="SE-23450464-6eca-4bc5-8e5f-e8679d938f9b" style="color:#1b1c1d;">를 통해, 업계에서 가장 성공 확률이 높은 자산을 보유하고 있음을 입증했습니다.</span></p><p
